\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Four-\/state (0/\+1/\+\+X/\+Z) vector helpers for Metal simulation. 

Values are represented as \{val, xz\} where xz marks unknown or high-\/impedance bits. 

Definition in file \doxymbox{\hyperlink{gpga__4state_8h_source}{gpga\+\_\+4state.\+h}}.
